ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u8	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Ladies and gentlemen! I present on behalf of the Extraordinary Commission on amendments to the codifications a report on the government draft law amending the Code of Administrative Procedure Act and certain other laws, print Nos 1183 and 1304. As the applicants point out, the project aims to counter the chronicity of administrative proceedings and to increase the confidence of citizens in administrative authorities and their decisions. This is to be done by improving administrative procedures and shortening their duration, reducing the amount of casatorial decisions and a more partnership of administration towards citizens. A few words about the process of the project. Marshal of the Sejm at the 34th meeting of the Sejm on 25 January 2017 pursuant to Article. 87. 2 in conjunction with Article. 90. 1 of the Rules of Procedure of the Sejm referred this draft to the Extraordinary Commission for amendments to the codifications for consideration. The Commission considered the draft at its meetings on 25 January and 22 February 2017. During the course of the work, he was assigned to the Permanent Sub-Committee on the revision of the electoral code and the Administrative Procedure Code, which I had the honour of chairing. The subcommittee was held on 8 February. Written opinions for the bill were presented by the Public Prosecutor General of the State Treasury and the National Representation of Self-Governmental Colleges of Appeals. The latter opinion consisted of the positions of the SKO from Gdańsk and Łódź. The meetings of the committees and subcommittees were attended by the Undersecretary of State in the Ministry of Development, Mariusz Haladyj, together with his colleagues, committee experts, as well as employees of the Sejm: the secretariat of the Committee of Justice, the Extraordinary Commission and the Legislative Office. In addition, Kamil Szmid, together with his colleagues, took part in the meeting of the committee. The result of the committee's work is the draft law included in the committee's report in print No 1304. At the meeting of the subcommittee and committee, general discussions were held on the project and then all the regulations of the project were systematically discussed and adopted twice. In the opinions submitted and during the discussion, doubts were raised as to the appropriateness of some of the project regulations. As a result of the discussion and the amendments notified, some of these doubts were eliminated or mitigated. Six substantive amendments were adopted at the subcommittee meeting, [[Game in the O.R., bell ringing.]] and additionally legislative amendments. The substantive amendments concerned the clarification of certain issues, including: who may be a participant in mediation, as well as defining the mediator's powers in accessing the file. The next eight substantive amendments and legislative amendments were adopted at the committee meeting. These included: that the authority should not only instruct about the right to waive the appeal itself, but also about the consequences of such a waiver, and that it should specify that a waiver of the imposition of an administrative penalty will be possible in the case of the previous final punishment of the party for the same administrative penalty or penalty provided for in criminal or criminal law. Practically alone, the proposal for the preservation of the article was not accepted. 15 c.p.a. in the current version, i.e. that the administrative procedure is two-way. He has overcome the view that this provision should be adapted to the existing legal situation and to the existing exceptions, i.e. The wording of the text shall be: "The administrative procedure shall be two-way unless otherwise provided for in the specific provision. The work of the committee, High Izbo, was constructive, efficient and carried out in a good atmosphere. This was seen in the votes. At the subcommittee meeting, the draft was adopted unanimously, no one was against, no one abstained. The same was true at the committee's meeting: 8 Members voted in favour of the report, no one opposed it, and no one abstained. The Extraordinary Commission for Codification Changes after considering the draft law asks the High Sejm to adopt the draft law as proposed in print 1304. I would like to thank the ladies and gentlemen for their cooperation, as well as all the experts and participants in the committee's work. In the event of specific questions, I shall remain at the disposal of the High House. [[Applause]] [[President of the session includes Deputy Marshal Joachim Brudziński]]

There is no doubt that for the economic development of Poland, changes in administrative procedures are necessary to improve the assessment of Poles' functioning of administration. The vast majority of us, both businessmen and other citizens, find the present state unacceptable because of formalism, because of the complexity of procedures, because of the length of proceedings, because of the lack of confidence in pending decisions. That is why we strongly agree with the aim of the Council of Ministers to prepare this project. This is to combat the long-term nature of administrative proceedings and to increase citizens' confidence in administrative authorities and administrative courts and their decisions. As a result, we also support the implementation of the specific objectives set out by the Council of Ministers: improving administrative procedures and shortening their duration, reducing the amount of casatorial decisions, a more partnership of administration towards citizens. In our view, the project contains a number of solutions leading to the objectives pursued: to resolve factual and legal doubts in favour of the party, to seek an amicable settlement of the matter. The project makes it possible to use mediation in the administration, provides for new clear rules for the application of administrative penalties, which should be appropriate to the offence and the accompanying circumstances, and, finally, shortens the way for administrative cases in administrative courts. We want a more serviceable country for citizens, we want a faster examination of administrative matters, we want a more efficient administration. Therefore, on behalf of the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice, I declare that the club will support further work on the bill. Thank you. [[Applause]]
ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u11	Thank you. Mrs Katarzyna Osos, please take the floor.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u12	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour to present on behalf of the Civic Platform Club a position on the Extraordinary Commission's report on the draft law amending the Code of Administrative Procedure Act and some other laws. The proposed changes from the point of view of the citizen, individuals should be assessed positively, which was already the case during the first reading of the project, when all clubs agreed to direct the project to further work. High Izbo! The administrative procedure covers a very wide range of cases, and it is in this procedure that citizens' cases are most often dealt with. That is why it is so important that administrative authorities act efficiently, quickly and that regulations, on the one hand, guarantee the protection of individual rights and, on the other hand, uphold the legitimate public interest. And it has to be acknowledged that the proposed changes are moving in this direction, because we have here solutions to deformise and speed up the procedure, we have a whole package of solutions related to the amicable settlement of a case or solutions that aim to eliminate excessive use of casatorial powers, which significantly prolonged the procedure and was quite common practice. It is also important for an individual to introduce a principle of dispute settlement in favour of the party where there are doubts about the content of the legal standard and the subject matter of the proceedings is the imposition on the party of an obligation or restriction or withdrawal of the right. Such regulation will certainly safeguard the interests of the parties against excessive discretion in the interpretation of the law by employees of public administrations. It is also a positive change that the principle that public administrations do not depart from a well-established practice of resolving cases in the same factual and legal situation without unjustified reasons. Consequently, the party to the proceedings requesting the initiation of the procedure will be able to foresee a possible decision in advance, in so far as the factual or legal situation of the party concerned is such alone as previously decided. However, there is a problem with this regulation. the fact that it does not define the vague concept of established case-solving practice. Negatively, the proposed amendment to point 5 of the article should be assessed in our assessment. 1 of the project, namely the indication that administrative proceedings may not be of a two-way nature in the situations referred to in the specific provisions. Abstracting from constitutional arguments, because there is no uniformity of views on constitutionality or lack of constitutionality in terms of the necessity of two-instant administrative proceedings, attention should be paid to the important arguments of both legal and practical nature. Firstly, the absence of a two-instant nature of the administrative procedure may not only result in the parties being deprived of the right to review the case twice, but also, in fact, detract from the operation of the so-called. administrative supervision of lower-level bodies carried out by higher-level bodies. Secondly, such regulation may aggravate differences in the practice of applying certain provisions where higher authorities are deprived of the possibility of verifying such decisions. Thirdly, such regulation will ultimately lead to an avalanche increase in complaint cases to the administrative court, which is in fact more formalised, time-consuming and costly. During the committee's work, an amendment was tabled concerning the deletion of this proposal, but it was not accepted by the majority. The other amendments proposed during the committee's work should be assessed positively. Although the project designer has not avoided some oversimplifications, the global assessment of the project submitted must remain positive. The Civic Platform will therefore vote in favour of this report. Thank you. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u16	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! Minister! On behalf of the Poselski Club I am proud to present my position on the report of the Extraordinary Commission on amendments to the codifications of the Government draft law amending the Code of Administrative Procedure Act and some other laws, print No 1304. There is no doubt that amendments to the Code of Administrative Procedure aimed at introducing solutions to eliminate excessive formalism from administrative proceedings, which resulted in a significant length of proceedings, should be considered appropriate. In particular, the proposed solutions to bring about an amicable settlement through mediation deserve support. There is no doubt that mediation is under-used in our legal culture, so it is good that this project introduces the possibility of dealing with matters in this form, including in the case of administrative matters. It is only necessary to wish that the practice of these cases should lead to the highest possible number of administrative cases by mediation. Solutions to speed up administrative procedures as well as the wider use in administrative procedures of the principle of convincing citizens to govern administrative authorities are also welcome. The introduction of a general rule on the settlement of legal doubts and the rules on the settlement of factual doubts in favour of the party in cases where an obligation is to be imposed on the party or its entitlement is to be taken away should also be welcomed. Attention and support should also be given to solutions to speed up and formalise administrative procedures by introducing rules on the silent settlement of the case or simplified procedure, as well as the possibility to object to casatorial decisions, which will potentially eliminate the abuse of the institution by administrative authorities. On the other hand, our doubts are raised by the wording of the article. 15 - amendment of Article in principle. 15 - the Code of Administrative Procedure, which introduces the possibility of limiting the principle of duality in administrative proceedings. This is all the more questionable because of the wording of the article. 14, which requires ministers to verify all administrative proceedings with a view to the appropriateness of maintaining a two-way procedure or its possible limitations. It is in our view that it may violate the constitutional principles of duality. However, given that further specific provisions Or they will refer to this principle, we will monitor very closely and in detail those laws which will eventually be brought forward, in the course of further work on specific laws and here, however, ensure that this principle of duality is not completely eliminated. Because, as it was said here, the ruling of cases also requires the possibility of being verified by a higher authority, as well as by a higher authority, to have full control over the settlement of cases in the bodies of first instance, so it can interfere here in situations where such case law is inappropriate and thus shape a line of case law in administrative proceedings. So, in view of all these aspects, i.e. the decisive advantage of the positives over the negatives of this law, the Posel Club The modern will support this government presentation. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u22	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Art. 15 is replaced by the following: Administrative proceedings shall be two-way unless otherwise provided for in the specific provision. The explanatory memorandum states that the aim of the project is not to repeal the principle of duality. Allowing the administrative procedure to be shortened to one instance does not necessarily mean reducing the duration of the whole process. The limitation of the right to appeal by administrative means will undoubtedly result in an increase in the number of complaints before the administrative court. Judicial proceedings are seen by the citizen as being ailing. It is more formalized - pleadings, longer - long time periods of trial, and also involves the payment of costs - court fees, ordering the case to a professional proxy. Thus, the citizen is deprived of a relatively simple, cheap and proven mechanism of questioning the decisions of the state authorities. Are you sure this change has been introduced in the interests of the citizen and is helpful in his fight against the country's machine? Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

That is why today we are proposing an amendment that reduces this population threshold to 1 million. We believe that in Silesia there will be enough local governments who want to integrate and jointly pursue common and ambitious goals to reach this million-dollar electoral threshold. However, thanks to the introduction of a millionth threshold into the bill, we will certainly avoid these dangers which I have been talking about, namely, firstly, pulling local governments into power which do not want to integrate as strongly as the law requires, secondly, temptations to reach the threshold of the need to attract all local authorities, often infrastructureally and structurally unrelated to the center of the agglomeration, and this can unfortunately cause problems in the future related to the management of this area.

We are convinced that the people of the future metropolis will benefit from the changes introduced by the law. The Silesian-Załębian integrated body will become a source of building a competitive advantage in Europe and will directly affect the quality of life of the inhabitants of the Silesian voivodship. The definition of the tasks of the metropolitan area and the elimination of questionable provisions concerning the formation of metropolitan areas by municipalities, rather than more generally by local authorities, is fundamental, as the doubts related to the interpretation of these issues could paralyze the functioning of the new organism. However, existing forms of cooperation could not form a metropolis in the full sense of the word. They had integrative power, they were the place of exchange of opinions, decision-making on the division of tasks, decision on the priority of investment, but they did not have the power to integrate processes e.g. in the field of collective transport or spatial management. At the time of the entry into force of this law, this situation will change positively. For the modern idea of a metropolis is an idea of intensive development that is worth supporting. It is worth supporting not only in relation to the Silesian voivodship, but also other metropolitan organisms in Poland such as Warsaw, Wrocław, Tricity or Poznań. Each of these organisms has its own specific characteristics, which is why it would not always be justified to move directly from the legislation voted on today. This law is - let it be - a kind of pilot in this area. Observing the first experience of Silesian and subterranean local governments creating a metropolis for conurbations, i.e. cities of a dispersed nature, without a strict centre, we will be richer with knowledge which should be used to create solutions for a strictly defined center of agglomeration. The Union is to carry out public tasks in the field of spatial governance, social and economic development, the area of planning, coordination and integration and the development of public collective transport, including road, rail and other rail transport, as well as sustainable urban mobility, metropolitan passenger transport, cooperation in determining the course of national and voivodeship roads in the area of the union and promoting the relationship and its area. In line with the intention expressed in the explanatory memorandum, the project is intended to carry out development, integration and coordination tasks while reducing the risks associated with the decommissioning and transformation of the organisations concerned so far. This will enable the adopted model of the union to allow for its evolutionary transformation on the basis of voluntary agreements on the taking over of municipal, district or voivodship tasks. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u70	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Literally a few comments from me as rapporteur for the questions that have been addressed to the government here. Some of my comments will once again be such a shortcut and a repetition of the argument concerning this specific solution for the Śląskie voivodship. Firstly, I would like to point out to ladies and gentlemen that the set of instruments contained in the draft law on the creation of a metropolitan union in the Silesian Voivodeship, which is intended to facilitate the management of cities, in the case of Silesia of dozens of cities on the rights of the county, is very well-diagnosed by the government, to the extent that the particular urban and administrative structure of this Silesian conurbation is emphasized here. Therefore, the expectation that the same instruments which have been included in the Act, which is repealed by this law on the creation of a metropolitan union, serve to metropolitanise other areas, this is a misunderstanding. I would like to remind you that - except maybe Warsaw - postulates or, let's say, some public statements concerning metropolitan initiatives sometimes refer primarily to a centralised structure, where there is one central, dominant city, which is surrounded by small municipalities. This is a completely different situation than in the area we have in Silesia, in the part of Silesian Voivodeship - several cities on the rights of the county around the capital of the region, which is Katowice. I would also like to stress that the instruments contained in the law, in quotes, in Silesia, dedicated to this area, are an expected response for many years to these huge and specific problems of the region, which cannot be ignored, because on the basis of these various sentiments they were and are built of various kinds, in quotes, quasi-recepts to return to sometimes very intimately understood, historically functioning solutions. Third note. I also want to write very strongly to make it sound very clear that, because we have a shortage of statutory instruments for integrating development policy in the area of cities strongly linked to each other, from the point of view, I would say infrastructure, road, you can say, building intensity, etc., despite this fact, we also have very large disparities in the area of Silesian cities. This has not been said at second reading, but I would point out that even in those cities that will be covered by the metropolitan union, there is a difference, a discrepancy in the scale of unemployment, that is, it can be said, on the extreme example, from 5 and 6 points e.g. in Katowice up to 17% in Bytom. So that this law, by giving some tools to build a balance in development, integration and cooperation, above all at the statutory level, is also intended to abolish these differences - it is about a certain, I would say, balance in investment, in a closer, integrated area of combating unemployment or equalising quality and standard of living. These are issues that are very strongly highlighted by local governments of Silesian agglomerations and Zagłębian cities that participate in this project. And it is worth noting that this government project is also an expression of full confidence in this process, which has already taken place in the last dozen years. After all, it is worth noting in this public debate that even in the 1990s, without having in the current legal conditions the local government solutions which were subsequently introduced in 1999 and earlier, in the mid-1990s, in the former Katowice voivodeship, in order to respond in any way to this rapidly changing social and economic situation, the government concluded a contract for the Katowice voivodship, which is worth recalling today in this debate. To sum up, the law on the creation of a metropolitan union in the Śląskie voivodship creates very durable, stable and above all rational perspectives for this area and is thinking about the future, is thinking about the common good. We are talking about a major legislative proposal that integrates more than 2 million people. If we make simplified proposals for this, you can say, a very significant population limit, I imagine that, for example. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u72	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! The draft law amending certain laws in connection with the tasks of public administrations in the field of certain public registers, which I am pleased to present, was developed jointly by the Ministry of Digitisation and the Ministry of Family, Labour and Social Policy and introduces changes that will enable citizens to handle a number of official matters in a simpler and easier way through the use of ICT systems. The starting point for drawing up this project, as well as other changes expressed by the government in this area, is that, since the State has collected in public systems certain information about the citizen, his situation or rights and obligations, it should use them of its own motion and not burden him with the obligation to provide certificates for these circumstances. It is worth recalling that according to the article. 220 of the Code of Administrative Procedure, a public administration body may not require a certificate or a statement of fact or legal status, if it is known to the body of its own motion, to be established by the authority on the basis of its records of registers or other data, public registers held by other public entities to which it has access, and as a result of the exchange of information with another public body. Unfortunately, this practice and, above all, the experience of citizens show that these principles are actually applied differently and sometimes they are not applied. The aim of the government, and in particular the Minister for Digitalisation, is to organise the ICT systems in which public registers are kept in order to maximise the efficiency of day-to-day handling by citizens using electronic means. The submitted project involves rebureaucratizing several procedures: from information about the number of collected so-called. Penalty points in the driver's account to formalise family benefits proceedings. The bill consists of two parts. The first includes changes to the road traffic legislation in the area of: firstly, allowing the Internet to check the number of points assigned to the driver for the infringements committed, secondly, allowing free access to vehicle data collected in the central vehicle register to the environmental inspection bodies and, finally, the changes necessary for the smooth operation of the CEPiK system, including. by adding the possibility to collect data on referrals to medical and psychologically tested persons who hold or have withdrawn power to drive motor vehicles, persons who are not entitled to drive the vehicle who have committed an infringement and persons who are not entitled to a criminal measure in the form of a ban on driving. We propose that information on the number of criminal points be made available after authentication of the data subject with a trusted profile on the ePUAP platform. As part of this e-service, the user concerned will be presented with up-to-date information about the points so-called. final assigned for infringements of traffic rules on the basis of the final judgment of the court, the criminal mandate, the order of the court on the conditional dismissal of proceedings or the judgment of the court on the infringement in disciplinary proceedings, as well as the so-called paragraphs. temporary, i.e. those that will ultimately be assigned in case of confirmation of an infringement by a final decision. At this stage, e-service will not present the data of the person whose driving rights have been revoked, who has been banned from driving or whose points have expired. In addition, the project provides for free access to vehicle data collected in the central vehicle register to environmental inspection bodies. This is a response to the demands of inspections in this area, which include, inter alia, carrying out the tasks set out in the rules on international shipments of waste. Among the reported cases of illegal shipments of waste there are numerous cases of illegal exports of waste in the form of vehicles from Poland, as well as waste from these vehicles. In the case of exports of such waste abroad, the liability for its illegal movement shall be borne by the seller of a vehicle under the jurisdiction of the Republic of Poland. It is therefore important that the Chief Environmental Inspector has the data, the data of the last owner of the vehicle, which will enable proceedings to be conducted in this respect. Otherwise, the costs associated with the proper management of the retained waste will have to be borne by the Chief Environmental Officer, which follows from the provisions of the Regulation of the European Parliament and of the Council on shipments of waste. In addition, on the basis of Article. Article 17 (1) (a) and (b) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 1 of the Act of 20 January 2005 on the Recycling of End-of-Life Vehicles, the Chief Environmental Inspector shall conduct administrative proceedings in matters relating to the clearance of entities introducing vehicles from the obligation to provide a collection network. In the above proceedings, the Chief Environmental Inspector assumes that the introduction of a vehicle for the purpose of settlement of the lack of network charge shall take place at the time of the sale of the vehicle. It is therefore necessary to fix the date of first registration of vehicles introduced into the territory of the country. The amendment allows the main environmental inspector to obtain this data from the central vehicle register. The second, no less important, part of the project are changes in the rules on the implementation of family benefits, social assistance benefits, maintenance fund benefits, educational benefits and the Large Family Charter. The first facilitations for family benefits and maintenance fund benefits were introduced in 2016. They do not require applicants to accompany their application for income certificates or disability certificates. The authorities granting these benefits themselves through the electronic systems shall obtain the necessary information in this respect from the relevant registers. These arrangements have also been adopted in relation to the educational provision. When submitting this proposal, we propose that the next step be made available to the authorities performing the benefits to the family of further registers in order to determine their own rights to individual benefits. The real change that results from the bill submitted is that the family will not have to join the proposal, e.g. children's education certificates at school or high school. Following the entry into force of the provisions, it will be sufficient for the applicant to make an appropriate statement in this regard. Such a solution will also significantly reduce the bureaucratic burden on schools, since it will avoid the need for them to issue certificates. We estimate that the entry into force of these rules will result in the number of requests to the educational information system to reach around 2700 000 in the course of the year. This shows the scale of certification, which in other cases today schools must and must issue to those who apply for family benefits. At the same time, in order to eliminate irregularities, the authority determining the right to individual benefits by electronic means, through the IT systems of the Minister responsible for Family Affairs, shall obtain information from the individual registers to verify the correctness of the statements made. The detailed arrangements proposed in the draft law are to allow independent access to: educational information system - to bodies granting family benefits, maintenance fund benefits, the Large Family Charter and the provision of education, the central list of insured persons - to bodies granting family benefits, maintenance fund benefits and education benefits, data collected in the insured person's account and the payer's account of contributions under social security rules - to bodies granting family benefits, social assistance benefits, maintenance fund benefits and educational benefits, as well as the PESEL register - to bodies granting the Large Family Card and the EXMOoN system - to bodies granting the Large Family Card. In addition, it is envisaged that the Minister of Family, Labour and Social Policy can obtain access to the data contained in those registers in order to monitor how these benefits are implemented by the competent authorities. We also propose that the authorities carrying out the provision of information on the accounting of a person from personal income tax should be able to verify compliance with the condition of residence in the territory of the Republic of Poland. We want the law to enter into force on 1 July 2017, with the exception of the provisions on the online exercise of the possibility of checking the number of points received by the driver for traffic violations committed, because we want these provisions to enter into force on 1 April 2017 - this is a matter of such importance that the number of persons who, unfortunately, exceed the rules of road traffic law in the course of the year is close to 3.5 million, and this is a service generally expected - secondly, the provisions concerning the transmission by the Police of information to the central register of drivers of data collected in the register of drivers violating traffic regulations - we want this change to enter into force on the day following the date of the announcement - the rules for obtaining information from the educational information system by the Minister responsible for family affairs information necessary to determine the right to benefits for monitoring these benefits - we want these provisions to enter into force 14 days after the announcement - and finally the amendments to the Act on the educational information system for which it was foreseen to enter into force on 1 January 2018 due to the need to adapt the software also to the revised structure of schools. In presenting the above, I kindly ask the High House to adopt the bill submitted. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u74	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour on behalf of the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice to present to the High House a position on the government draft law amending certain laws in connection with the tasks of public administrations in the field of certain public registers, Parliamentary printing No 1305. More generally, the bill concerns the introduction of changes enabling the citizen to deal with a whole range of public administration issues without having to leave the house, i.e. through ICT systems, but not only. These changes are the next step in the implementation of the government project from paper to digital Poland, coordinated by the Minister of Digitization. The government project proposes changes to a whole range of laws covering two areas. The first area covers traffic regulations, while the second one covers the implementation of various types of benefits, including family benefits, social assistance benefits, maintenance, health benefits, educational benefits or benefits under the Large Family Charter. As far as area one is concerned, the project creates a legal framework for e-services for citizens to check online up-to-date information on the data collected in the central register of drivers. The intended service and the scope of the data to be provided shall correspond to the scope of the data which are included in the certificates issued by the Police when the driver requests such a certificate directly to the Police. The draft law also allows free access to vehicle data collected in the central vehicle register to environmental inspection bodies. The Chief Environmental Inspector has repeatedly requested access to data in connection with the illegal shipment of waste in the form of vehicles or parts thereof, but has until now been unauthorised to obtain data and information collected in the central vehicle register, thereby preventing him from carrying out his statutory duties. So much for area one. As far as area two is concerned - let's call it the area of benefits - it includes changes in 10 laws. It's worth remembering. These are the Act on Professional and Social Rehabilitation, the Act on Social Insurance, the Act on Family Benefits, the Act on Social Assistance, the Act on Health Care, the Law on Higher Education, the Act on Assistance to Persons with Child Support, the Act on the System of Educational Information, the Act on the Charter of the Large Family and the Law on State Aid to Raising Children. The nature of the proposed changes is a radical reduction in the number of certificates required, which must be attached to the applicant's application each time. Individual bodies carrying out various types of benefits, e.g. family, educational, health, resulting from the Charter of the Large Family, will have independent access to the information needed, as Mr Zagórski said extensively. As a result, citizens will not have to obtain this information on their own, which will, of course, reduce the bureaucratic burden in this regard. Mr Zagórski spoke very comprehensively about the details. I will conclude by saying that the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice supports the draft law submitted by the government to amend certain laws in connection with the tasks of public administrations in the field of certain public registers, Parliamentary printing No 1305. The parliamentary club supports this government project in its entirety. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

Designed changes are a continuation and development of existing solutions. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u113	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! I am pleased to present to the High House a bill amending the Social Security Act and the Act amending the Labour Code Act and some other laws. You can say that the project assumes the implementation of a program or e-Składka project. The main objective of the project is to introduce a uniform standard for the identification of the payer of the contributions by granting each payer an individual account number to which contributions to the broadly understood social security will be transferred, as well as health insurance contributions, contributions to the Labour Fund, the Guaranteed Occupational Benefits Fund and the Post-Most Pension Fund. The project covers about 7 million contributors to social security contributions, health insurance and other funds. It can be said that the aim of the project is to reduce the number of transfers, operations carried out in connection with the implementation of the social security obligation. On a yearly basis, according to 2015 data, the number of contributions made by payers to individual funds is over 65 million, of which 21.5 million are payments made to the Social Insurance Fund, 27 million are payments made for health insurance, 16.5 million are payments made to the Labour Fund and the Guaranteed Occupational Benefits Fund and 160 000. These are payments made to the Post-Most Pension Fund. In the current legal state, premium payers are obliged to pay contributions to separate bank accounts, as indicated by the undertaking, broken down by title. These payments shall be made through payment documents dedicated to the payment of contributions. The documents shall have dedicated fields identifying the contributions and the payer of the contributions. As regards the instruments for identifying the payer, the so-called contributions are used. Numeric identifiers - alternatively NIP, REGON, PESEL - or ID number. The payment of contributions is now required to also indicate the period for which the contribution is paid by specifying the month, year and number of the billing declaration. Contributions go to four central premium accounts, i.e. the social security account, the health insurance bill, the account for the Guaranteed Occupational Benefits Fund and the Labour Fund, as well as the account for the Post-Madder Pension Fund. In the case of an erroneously entered identifier, it is not possible to automatically identify the paid contribution with the payer's account. The Social Insurance Institution must then carry out an investigation procedure which requires cooperation not only with the payer of the contributions but also with the institution serving the contribution, i.e. with a bank, postal operator or other payment institution. As regards data from January 2016, the Social Insurance Institution received around 281 thousand during that period. unidentified payments for the amount of PLN 328 million and in each individual case undertake such an investigation. As regards the substance of the proposed amendment, it implies the introduction of the principle of identification of contributions from the undertaking after the individual premium account number to be allocated to each of the payers. This will significantly reduce the number of investigations conducted by the undertaking in this area, eliminate the impact of new, unidentified contributions. The draft law aims to replace the existing system of collection of contributions, based on four central premium accounts, with the system of individual premium accounts generated for contributors directly by the undertaking. The individual contribution accounts for contributors generated by the undertaking will only be used to record contributions, as if they would not have a wider scope of application, in particular they would not be payment accounts, i.e. no other operations, in particular payments, may be made out of these accounts. The Social Insurance Institution will be authorised to assign its own numbering according to the IBAN Account Standard. There will be a change in the identifier for the payer of the contributions by assigning the individual account numbers of the contribution accounts, which will become new identifiers, and the handling of generated premium accounts will be possible by any entity, in particular the bank. This project will enable contributors to reduce the number of payments made and reduce the amount of information given on payment documents. Payments will be made by only one transfer in place of the four existing ones, in the form of a standard Elixir message, to the individual account number generated by the bet for each payer. This will reduce the number of payments to be made by the IT systems of banks and the establishment on a yearly basis from 65 million, as has been the case so far, to 27 million, i.e. to the number of potential health insurance contributions based on 2015 data. As regards the advantages of the proposed changes, as I mentioned, they include simplifying the rules for the identification of contributions at the payer's level, as well as rapid access to information on the balance of the account for the payer and the insured, simplifying the system of payment for contributions, clearing payments for the oldest arrears, applying modern financial solutions or using the instrument of direct debit. The draft provides that the bet will generate the contribution account number for the payer of the contribution as soon as the payer's contribution account has been created. As regards payers who already have an account in the Social Insurance Institution, such account numbers will be generated by the establishment by 31 December 2017. The Social Insurance Institution will be required to inform the payers of the number by that date and, in the absence of this obligation, the payer will be obliged to request the Social Insurance Institution for information on the number of the contribution account that was assigned to them. The bet will have 3 months to fulfil this obligation, i.e. period from October to December 2017 The proposed solution will fundamentally change the debt structure of the ZUS contributions - and this is actually the third advantage of this new solution - by accounting for current receipts for the oldest debts. There is a debt phenomenon in the social security financial system. As regards the level of this debt for the years 1999 to 2011, it currently amounts to PLN 10 800 million, of which its share in the debt of active payers, as of October 2016, amounts to PLN 2 billion. The amount of debt is about 480 thousand. active contributors, that is about 30% debtors, for the total number of 1300 thousand. debtors. Current revenue on contributions receivable amounts to more than PLN 12 billion per month, if we are talking about the impact on the Social Insurance Fund, and are made by payers who do not have a debt of nearly PLN 10 billion. This applies to around 800,000. payers. Only for this group of payers who do not have debt, the introduction of a new principle for the settlement of contributions will have no impact, but for the others, who can be said to be affected by the debt phenomenon, each subsequent contribution will be counted against the oldest arrears. The use of the oldest payment algorithm in the settlement of contributions may result in the payment of contributions in the event of debt in the payer's account not being settled against current contributions. For the insured person, this may mean no contributions - because as a consequence, you can say, the oldest periods have been settled, the premiums have been paid to cover the insured premiums declared during that settlement period - or otherwise the insured person will be covered by contributions together with interest for late payment for the oldest period at the expense of current debt. At the same time, it should be noted that the rules already in force do not provide for the possibility of determining the payment for the payment of claims for a particular insured person. Thus, any underpayment of the current contribution results in debt arising also from those insured against whom the payer has deducted contributions from full remuneration. Under the rules in force, the payer shall decide on the amounts to which the contributions are paid instead of the insured. Therefore, the amounts collected from his remuneration may already be settled for the benefit of other insured persons. Here it is worth noting that the debt of the current period is not at risk of statute of limitations and is therefore possible to cover or repay future payments. Thus, the coverage of the missing contributions will be spread over time. In view of the contributions due for the period from January 2017, the limitation period will be 5 years, so that on the date of entry into force of the proposed provisions the undertaking will have 4 years to take action to enforce the claim. With regard to the Social Insurance Fund and this debt, which I mentioned, this situation may affect nearly 1300 thousand in 1999 and 2011. insured. To conclude, I would like to draw attention to three solutions, which are also part of this project, and which in this sense relate to matter, which is being amended by the bill presented. Namely, I mean introducing or securing the rights of insured persons in the event of the determination of the ineffective transfer of insured persons under Article. The 23rd Labour Code. It is proposed here to add in Article. 4 Act of the new provision, point 2a, which would contain a definition of so-called. the applicant for insurance as well as the addition after Article. 38 - today's law on the social security system of the new provision, i.e. Article 2 38a. This is a provision which, on the one hand, would give or could actually confirm the existing practice in this regard - the possibility for the pension authorities of the Social Security Office to issue decisions determining who, in such a case, in the event of such an ineffective transfer of workers together with a part or the whole of the work establishment under Article. 23, he's just a premium payer. The point is that doubts can be resolved in this regard. In this respect, it is also proposed to introduce a new solution in the proposed article. Article 38a (1) (a) (ii) (ii) (ii) (ii) (iii) (ii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii)) (ii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii)) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) (iii) 4 which would give the Social Insurance Institution, in the event of a criminal offence, the phenomenon of outsourcing of workers, such an unfair, ineffective outsourcing of workers, the possibility of making payments for social security contributions paid by such potential outsourcing for contributions to the former employer, the employer who was unsuccessfully transferred in accordance with Article. 23 The Labour Code, or part of it, and as a consequence, it can be said that there has been an individual change on the employer's side in employment relations with workers employed in the workplace or in part of it. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u115	Mrs. Marshal! Minister! High Seym! The purpose of this Act is to replace the current system of collection of contributions, based on four central premium accounts, with the system of individual premium accounts generated for contributors directly by the undertaking. This will improve the identification of payments resulting from the allocation of an individual account number to a specific payer of contributions. At present, each month it makes several payments to different bank accounts, resulting in costly and time-consuming contributions. In addition, investigations are currently under way to identify wrongly or incorrectly described contributions. The introduction of the principle of identification of contributions made by an undertaking after the individual premium account number will significantly reduce the number of investigations. This project will allow contributors to reduce the number of payments made and reduce the amount of information given on payment documents. Payments will be made by one transfer. This will also reduce the number of payments to be made to the processing by the IT systems of banks and the plant, which will be reduced from 58 million to around 27 million per year. The adoption of the solutions proposed in the draft law will allow the identification of contributions at the level of the payer, and will enable the automatic identification of contributors making contributions from abroad, and currently there are about 5 000. such deposits that are operated manually. In addition, the payer and the insured will have faster access to information about the balance of the account. The clearing system will be simplified using modern financial solutions. The project envisages that the establishment will be responsible for allocating funds between individual funds and their managers. In addition, the purpose of this draft law is to clarify certain provisions in order to eliminate differences in interpretation. Given that the proposed amendments aim to increase the efficiency and effectiveness of the Social Insurance Institution, the Law and Justice Club supports this project. Thank you very much. After the amendment of the law, each payer will make a single payment for the contribution to each payer by the Social Security Office, which will also serve to identify the payer of the contribution. It also allows the generation of payment documents according to the payer's identification data stored in the ZUS account. There will be a change in the payment settlement algorithm, which will consist in clearing them, starting with the oldest payments. This way of accounting will limit the limitation of premium claims. Currently, the payer of the contributions has the possibility to mark the payments up to the month for which the contributions are due. The proposed change will reduce the possibility of paying contributions for the period in accordance with the payer's availability of contributions. Currently, a person pursuing non-agricultural economic activities, subject to voluntary sickness insurance, who has arrears in paying contributions for earlier periods, but who pays contributions for current periods in time and in full in the case of sickness or maternity, shall be entitled to the payment of short-term sickness benefits. The proposed change will deprive such a person of the right to pay these benefits. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! This bill raises many questions, which is why the Civic Platform Parliamentary Club proposes that the project be directed to further work in the Committee on Social Policy and Family Affairs. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u135	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! First of all, I would like to thank you for the votes in the discussion, for the numerous questions. In fact, I could say that there is no doubt that everyone agreed that there is a need to amend the existing rules for the settlement of premiums. Actually, notice, we are talking about the introduction of a new standard of services provided by the Social Insurance Institution, but a standard that we already know. We know him from life, because according to the standards that we propose in this bill, we already settle periodic charges for electricity charges or gas charges. Each of us has an individual account to pay such monthly payments. The government's intention is to ensure that this modern standard also applies in the area of public services provided by the Social Insurance Institution. Of course, all kinds of doubts raised in the discussion are understandable, because we are talking about the scale of changes that are incomparable to changes in other segments of the economy in which these modern forms of settlement already function. We talked about the fact that this would apply to about 7 million payers. As regards the response to the specific questions raised in the discussion, one of the questions concerned the timetable for the implementation of the project. As I mentioned, when presenting the bill, it assumes that the process of generating individual accounts for contributors will be spread over 3 months for the last 3 months of 2017, i.e. it will start in October and will end in December this year. You can say that this action will also be accompanied by an information campaign, i.e. The Social Insurance Institution will inform the payers through the media about the new standard of services, which will start to apply from 1 January 2018. The project assumes situations where such generation and transmission of information about the allocated contribution to the payer may not occur by the end of 2017 for various reasons. In such a case, it assumes that the payer, having already knowledge of the new settlement rules in force from 1 January 2018, will have a month to appear, i.e. by the end of January he will be able to ask the ZUS pension authority to provide him with such an individual bank account number. This monthly period is linked to the payment of contributions: contributions for insured persons providing work in January 2018 are paid at the beginning of February this year. So, as I said, this month period is such a safety valve here. On the other hand, of course, we also have in the draft the principle that the consequences of not granting such an individual account number or not informing about an individual account number for reasons attributable to the Social Insurance Institution cannot be borne by the payer himself. With regard to the day-to-day handling of those payers who will register in the Social Insurance Institution after the entry into force of the Act, in the register of payers, this type of assignment of an individual account number will follow up on an ongoing basis. The draft law proposes simply to limit itself to the wording: the pension authority shall immediately assign such individual bank account number to the payer. As far as the next questions are concerned, you have also asked about the effects, the few questions that were asked during the discussion concerned the financial consequences of this change. From our point of view, the fact that these financial consequences will consist in reducing the costs of transfers, transferring funds to the Social Insurance Institution is obvious, but I do not hide that we had a problem with accurately assessing these effects, because we did not have access to information on the amount of fees charged for transfers by banks. For this reason, any respect would be burdened with a certain amount of lack of precision. Thus, in the impact assessment, such a precise indication of the scale of the benefits resulting from the reduction in the number of transfers from four to, it can be said that one per month for each payer of contributions could simply not have occurred. You also asked how the cost of implementing the law was estimated. Here we rely on the data provided by the Social Insurance Institution. Of course, the introduction of this new standard of service offered by the Social Insurance Institution will require changes in the IT system of the Social Insurance Institution. According to the estimates of the Social Security Office, the cost of such changes, together with the costs associated with this information campaign which I mentioned, should not exceed the amount of PLN 10 million. There have been questions regarding the introduction of this principle that, in the event of outstanding contributions to the payer's account, the newly paid contributions will be counted against the oldest outstanding liabilities. That's actually how you define the essence of this solution. It can be said, with regard to pension insurance, pension insurance, actually accident insurance, that the introduction of this rule should not entail negative consequences for the insured person himself, while there is a problem regarding voluntary sickness insurance, in particular when the payer of the contributions is simultaneously insured. Let me remind you that the rule laid down in the Social Insurance Act applies, according to which, in the case of voluntary sickness insurance, the delay in payment of contributions to that insurance results in the cessation of voluntary insurance. You can say that this problem is known. I can declare that we will try to return to this issue during the legislative work in the committee and propose an optimal solution. Thank you very much.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u137	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! In the view of the applicants, today's method is expensive, because payments are made on several different accounts - this involves different account numbers, and in the case of wrongly entered data it triggers the entire explanatory procedure. According to the project, each entrepreneur is to have an individual contribution account. On this account he would make all charges to the Social Security Office. This will replace social, health contributions, the Labour Fund and the Guaranteed Occupational Benefits Fund and the Pension Fund, reduce the number of payments by half. The draft also provides for an amendment to the non-payment certificates. Legally, certificates issued in paper form and electronic printouts generated by the payer from ZUS systems were proposed. The proposed changes aim to increase the efficiency and effectiveness of the ZUS in carrying out tasks relevant to customers - contributors and insured persons. The PSL Club will be in favour of directing this project to further work on the committee. Thank you very much.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u138	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! I have the honour to present the position of the Polish Free and Solidarni Circle towards the government's draft law on the amendment of the Act on the Social Security System and the Act on the amendment of the Labour Code Act and some other laws. The level of bureaucracy in Poland in many institutions is absurd. Especially do Polish entrepreneurs struggle with it every day, who waste their time unnecessarily, fighting this heartless machine. The bill is intended to simplify the lives of almost 7 million Poles who pay contributions to various types of insurance. First of all, the proposed change will concern 3 million Polish contributors who will pay not only a few but only one contribution covering all obligations of the employer towards the Social Security Office. The Act will introduce a uniform standard for the identification of the payer of social security contributions, health insurance, the Labour Fund and the Guaranteed Occupational Benefits Fund - FP and FGŚP - and the Pension Fund - FEP - by introducing individual contributions accounts for the payers, to which a single payment will be made to all claims to which the Social Insurance Institution is obliged to collect. So far, the payer had to transfer money to contributions to three different ZUS accounts. In accordance with the novel he will send them to one individual account. With a large number of employees This task was time-consuming and resulted in high costs for transfers. This also resulted in countless errors related to the correct classification of contributions. The proposed amendment will make sure that the Social Security Office will not bear many costs related to explaining and investigating whose contributions and for what period they are in its account. The important thing is that the government in the draft law took care of the protection of the payer's contributions. Where the Social Security Office misdefines the individual number assigned, the payer shall have no consequences for this. I am pleased that such a big change will involve a wide-ranging information campaign, using national media - information programs, press articles, the use of Internet portals, etc. - and activities directly organized by ZUS institutions addressed to their clients. This will avoid many ambiguities with regard to the contested law. The purpose of this draft law is also to clarify certain provisions so as to eliminate differences in interpretation arising in the practical application of existing laws. That is why the project includes solutions concerning, among others, use of the Central Database of RCI PESEL by the ZUS or legal alignment of electronic printouts generated by a qualified person from the Platform of Electronic Services of the Social Insurance Institution with certificates issued by the ZUS in paper form. For us, taxpayers and ZUS payers, the important novelty enshrined in the Act is the harmonisation of the rules of conduct of tax authorities and ZUS in the process of issuing individual interpretations. The clarity of the rules and the inability of an official to interpret them is the basis for citizens' confidence in the state. This is a solution worthy of support. The Act also solves problems related to any interpretation of the provisions in relation to the backlog of bailiffs who, using data collected by the Social Security Office during executions, do not pay in time for the data made available. There is no doubt that the bailiff should pay a fee for providing such information. Today, the bailiff gets the data before paying the payment, which often means that he does not see the need to settle a liability to the Social Security Office. The Act provides for changes to determine the time limit within which the bailiff should pay the fee and gives the possibility to charge interest as a consequence of failing to meet the payment deadline. This is a fair and justified solution. It cannot be that the enforcement institution itself does not regulate them on time, using inaccurate rules. Given that the proposed solutions will simplify and harmonise the social security system, which is for the benefit of the citizens, the Free and Solidarni Circle requests that the law be further reviewed.

ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u140	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Committee on Culture and Media, I have the honour to present a report on the parliamentary draft resolution on honoring the memory of Professor Marian Raciborski on the 100th anniversary of his death, print No. 1273. Professor Marian Raciborski was an outstanding scientist, botanist, flora researcher, co-founder of Polish paleobotany and nature conservation movement in Poland. He was born on September 16, 1863 in the village of Brzóstowa near Mothmielowo. In 1881, he studied natural sciences and medicine at the Jagiellonian University and later continued his studies at universities in Germany. Between 1896 and 1900 he was in South-East Asia, where he studied the flora of the island of Java. During his stay on the island he created huge botanical collections, which he gave to Kraków's ethnographic museum. Some of his national collections were purchased for the Natural Museum. Dzieduszycki in Lviv. After returning to Poland from 1900 to 1909, the professor was the director of the Department of the Agricultural Academy in Dublana near Lviv. Since 1909, as a professor of botany at the University of Lviv, he organized the Biological and Botanical Institute at the university I mentioned. Since 1912 he was a professor of botany at the Jagiellonian University, where he founded the Botanical Institute in 1913 where he organized his own research school. He died in 1917 in Zakopane. Prof. Raciborski's scientific achievements put him in the order of the most versatile Polish scholars. His works concern floristics, algae, fungi, vascular plants, plant geography and paleobatics. The first Polish Paleobotanical works and the launch of the collective study of Flora Poland are of particular importance. The first volume of this study was released after the death of the professor in 1919. Professor Marian Raciborski was one of the pioneers of nature conservation in Poland. In 1910 he published a list of historic trees and plant communities. The first promoted the protection of natural monuments and the extension of natural and national museums. He also published greenery of okritonasienny plants and lower plants, which gained recognition in Poland and abroad. The Committee on Culture and Media, following its first reading and consideration of the draft at its meeting on 23 February, asks the High Sejm to adopt this draft resolution as follows: The resolution of the Polish Sejm on honoring the memory of Professor Marian Raciborski on the 100th anniversary of his death. On 17 March 2017 marks the 100th anniversary of the death of Marian Raciborski, an outstanding Polish scholar, father of Polish paleobotany, pioneer of the nature conservation movement in Poland. Marian Raciborski was born in 1863 in Brzóstowa near Ostrowiec Świętokrzyskiego. He has been interested in nature since his early years. This led him to undertake natural studies at the Jagiellonian University, which he then continued to do, among others. Bonn, Munich and Tybinga. After their completion he became an assistant at the Botanical Garden of the Jagiellonian University in Cracow. Between 1896 and 1900, Marian Raciborski conducted pioneer studies of the island of Java. This research has brought him international recognition and publicity. Thanks to his outstanding achievements, he became head of the Department of the Agricultural Academy in Dublana and later professor of botany at the University of Lviv and Jagiellonian University and director of the Botanical Garden of the Jagiellonian University in Cracow. At that time he was strongly involved in nature conservation activities in Poland, including the protection of the isolated species of Polish larch. His scientific work includes about 300 publications. Marian Raciborski was not only an outstanding scholar but also a great patriot. Raised in the family of the January insurgent, in 1914 he supported the wholehearted work of Józef Piłsudski and the legionary act. He didn't live to see the Free Country. He died on 17 March 1917 in Zakopane. On the 100th anniversary of the death of Professor Marian Raciborski, the Sejm of the Republic of Poland mentions his character and honors his memory and praises his scientific achievements and patriotic attitudes. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

The first promoted the idea of nature conservation and expansion of natural and national museums. In 1910 he printed a list of historic trees and vegetation collections. Raciborski's activities in the field of popularization of nature conservation issues contributed to the creation in Poland of a separate research discipline covered by academic lectures. World War I did not interrupt the work of the Institute or the activities of the Botanical Garden. 17 March 1917 Marian Raciborski dies in a sanatorium in Zakopane. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u150	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Raciborski was born in the memorable year 1863 - a year marked in Polish history by the January Uprising. In Krakow he started pioneering research on flora of previous geological epochs. He dealt mainly with fossil floras of Paleozoic and Mesozoic from the area of Poland. He developed one of the richest Jurassic plants in the world, which was discovered in Grojc near Krakow. Thanks to this research he gained the honorable title of the father of Polish paleobotany. His most important Paleobotanical works are: flora of Kraków refractory clays, retic flora in the Tatras and other fossil plants. During his stay in Java, he studied the flora of the western ferns of Java. During several days of expeditions, which covered vast areas of the island, he collected ferns. Within just six months, he wrote a paper describing 385 species of ferns, including 10 new, unknown science. These researches and discoveries brought Raciborski fame and recognition among scholars. In the second half of 1900 he returned to Poland with a well-established position and a rich scientific acquis. First, he sent all his wealth packed in 25 crates. It contains scientific collections: mushrooms, ferns and seed plants, as well as books, museum exhibits, and a collection of 81 dolls of puppet theatre (wajang-kaju) and shadow theatre (wajang-purwo) and a rich collection of weapons and everyday objects. In his pedagogical work he also initiated the so-called. botanical Thursdays, which means weekly meetings of naturalists of various disciplines. It is in them that the beginnings of the Polish Botanic Society established in 1922 should be seen. In 1912 the botanical Thursdays were moved to Kraków. Raciborski not only studied nature, but also fought for its protection. He was one of the pioneers of the nature conservation movement. He extended the concept of a natural monument to specimens of a dead nature: rocks, waterfalls, lakes. The first promoted the idea of nature conservation and expansion of natural and national museums. Raciborski left behind a very rich scientific work of about 300 publications. Its collective development of Flora Poland is of particular importance. In addition to Raciborski's publications, he also left several prosperous scientific centres, mostly created from scratch, rich museum collections gathered in Java, which he gave to the Krakow ethnographic museum. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u154	Thank you very much, sir. The Sejm agreed that in the discussion on this item it would hear five minutes' statements on behalf of clubs and circles. I'm opening a discussion. I would also ask Mr Mark Pole to speak on behalf of the Law and Justice Club.
ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u155	Thank you again. Ladies and gentlemen! High Izbo! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club, Law and Justice, I have the honour to present to you and your fellow Members our position on the government draft law amending the excise duty Act in print No 1288 and the report of the Committee on Public Finance, published in print No 1303. The main objective of the proposed project is to eliminate interpretation doubts in the legislation on excise duty exemptions for completely contaminated ethyl alcohol and the mutual recognition of the procedures for its contamination within the Member States of the Union, as referred to by both the Council Directive and the Regulation of the European Commission. The reasons for the submission of this regulation are irregularities related to the disinfection of totally contaminated ethyl alcohol by measures applied by one Member State which can easily be removed by eliminating the durability of its total contamination, which constitutes the basis for excise duty exemption. Therefore, only the production, acquisition of intra-Community alcohol and imports of ethyl alcohol totally contaminated with only measures authorised by all Member States of the European Union and additional measures authorised by the country in which the contamination occurs, as well as additional measures authorised by our country will result in exemption from excise duty. The draft also provides for continuous monitoring of the quantities of wholly contaminated alcohol purchased from the Member States of the Union by imposing an obligation on entities acquiring compulsory registration as taxable persons of excise duty to the competent tax authority and informing it of the planned quantity of the acquisition. It will also be necessary to determine the planned estimated consumption of completely contaminated alcohol and to indicate the places of business. It is worth noting at this point that the proposed amendments will not result in a tax obligation because completely contaminated ethyl alcohol is compulsoryly exempt from excise duty in all Member States of the European Union. Therefore, the proposed solutions will neither increase expenditure nor reduce revenue from the public finance sector, as the excise exemptions in question are in the current legal state and the proposed amendments are more specific. To sum up, our club is in favour of implementing the proposed amendments and will vote in favour of them, as recommended by the committee. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u157	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour of presenting on behalf of the Civic Platform Parliamentary Club a position on the government draft law amending the excise duty law. As my previous speaker said, EU law provides for the exemption from excise duty of wholly contaminated alcohol. This is regulated by the October 1992 Directive on the harmonisation of the structure of excise duties on alcohol and alcoholic beverages, commonly referred to as the Alcohol Directive in the slang of taxpayers. For alcohol contamination are used: a uniform disinfectant, so-called. the europolluter, or additional contaminating measures applied in the individual Member States. However, it must be said that some national contaminating measures, including in particular the Hungarian contaminater, are, firstly, unsustainable and also easy to remove, which facilitates tax fraud. In Poland there are many such frauds. At the same time, following the wording of the provisions, our courts allowed an interpretation that ethyl alcohol could be contaminated in any way permitted in any Member State, including the Hungarian method. Against this background, it should be pointed out that the regulation contained in the draft is currently being assessed and deserves a positive assessment, as it removes the interpretation doubts raised by the existing wording. Of course, it is in line with European Union law. According to the regulations contained in the project in the case of production in Poland the exemption will be subject to ethyl alcohol contaminated either by a eurocoagulant or by additional measures authorised by Poland, thus eliminating the facilitation of tax fraud. Therefore, as I said earlier, this project deserves a positive assessment. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u161	Dear Marshal! Dear Minister, High Izbo! On behalf of the Poselski Club I would like to present a statement concerning the Government's draft law amending the excise duty Act, in print no. 1288 and 1303. The project provides for the introduction into the excise tax law of solutions which are intended to eliminate fraud concerning the marketing of completely contaminated ethyl alcohol. The turnover of such alcohol would be subject to increased control. These changes would be achieved by eliminating interpretation doubts in the use of excise duty exemptions contrary to the objectives of the Alcohol Directive and the provision of Regulation No 162/2013 of the European Union Commission. According to the aforementioned ruling of the Supreme Administrative Court, alcohol contaminated by methods authorised in any European Union country, i.e. not only a euro-polluter, but also a virtuous Latvian or Hungarian, is exempt from excise duty. However, the amendment of the excise duty law will result in only alcohol contaminated by a europolluter or a Polish equivalent being released from excise duty in Poland, while it will still be possible to import alcohol contaminated by other contaminants from the European Union. This type of alcohol will therefore be authorised, but its production in Poland will be banned. This means that there will be some degree of restriction of freedom of economic activity, and what is important, this restriction will be discriminatory in relation to entrepreneurs from certain countries of the European Union. The amendment also imposes new burdens on entities involved in the acquisition of completely contaminated intra-Community alcohol, which according to the Ministry of Finance operate in Poland about 35. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u163	Mrs. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! The project mainly concerns the introduction of a uniform procedure for total alcohol contamination in the European Union and is therefore intended to address the doubts about the use of excise duty exemption in the case of completely contaminated ethyl alcohol. And here comes the definition of what it means to be completely contaminated, introduced by European Commission Directive 3199/93 on mutual recognition of procedures for the total contamination of ethyl alcohol for the purposes of exemption from excise duty. In general, however, the issue of excise duty exemption for ethyl alcohol is regulated. the October 1992 alcohol directive on the harmonisation of the structure of excise duties on alcohol and alcoholic beverages. The aim of the proposal is, as has already been emphasised here, to resolve the interpretative doubts arising from this directive. Once this right has been changed, the right to exemption from excise duty will be granted in respect of purely contaminated ethyl alcohol: in the case of a procedure applied by all Member States and the application of a contaminating agent, so-called. Europollutant, in the case of an additional procedure notified by the Member State and the application of additional contaminating measures applied in certain Member States. As has already been said, various measures have been applied so far. We used the Hungarian procedure. It turned out that they were unstable, easy to discard, which did not permanently secure contaminated alcohol. The draft law - I will add - requires operators to register as a taxable person excise duty and notify the proposed intra-Community acquisition to the tax authority. These reports, which are quite detailed, allow for the ongoing collection of information on ethyl alcohol purchased intra-Community and marketing of such alcohol. High Izbo! Today we deal with 9 days a month. Every citizen, entrepreneur who is obliged to pay taxes, has nine days when he should make a declaration of payment of the tax. It is appalling that the entrepreneur must ensure that by the seventh we pay PCC, the ZUS must be paid to the tenth if the business is carried out on its own, by the fifteenth - for example agricultural tax, to the twentieth - income tax, to the twenty-fifth - VAT.
