Ladies and gentlemen, what is the proposal to solve this problem? 'Cause that's the problem when an entrepreneur has to make sure that today's ten or nine or twelve or his deadline is over. There is a country in the world where this solution has been put into practice. This is Ireland. In Ireland, it is proposed, in fact, that all taxes should be settled by the 23rd day of the month following the month to which the accounts relate. What does the Kukiz Club propose? We are proposing a uniform tax day, i.e. a single time limit to which declarations should be made and the tax and various other tax charges should be settled accordingly, and that date would be the last day of the month following the month to which the accounts relate. And now what are the advantages of this solution? First of all, simplicity, simplicity and simplicity again. If, as I mentioned, 63 laws regulate this matter today, we are not able to eliminate these 63 provisions and replace one, but we can make 63 laws similar or the same, they will set a date for one day, the last day of the month, and that is what we propose. What are the benefits for citizens, for entrepreneurs? This is where I'm gonna focus on entrepreneurs. First of all, simplicity in tax settlement. On the other hand, we propose to postpone the tax settlement deadline in the shortest version by 5 days - this applies to VAT, while in the case of taxes such as PCC it is almost 3 weeks, in the case of ZUS settled today to the tenth it is about 20 additional days. How will this translate into business activity? In fact, thanks to this solution, we increase the tax liquidity of Polish entrepreneurs, Polish companies, companies that operate in Poland. And that's why we're prolonging, we're increasing their so-called. cash flow that money also has its price in time. Where large or medium-sized enterprises benefit e.g. from credit lines, they operate not only on the basis of their own resources, their own cash, but also on the basis of resources obtained e.g. from a bank in the form of credit lines, then, if such an entrepreneur does not have to pay the tenth or twenty-five, or the fifteenth, or the twentieth contribution to the Social Security Office or the advance payment on the tax or tax, the money will stay longer in his pocket, in his pocket, and, consequently, he will be able to trade it longer, so that his business costs will be reduced because such an entrepreneur will not have to borrow this money, for example. from the bank. Therefore, this is a solution which we consider to have the advantages itself, although it was signalled a problem that I would like to address immediately at the outset, because I suppose that from the plenary room from the mouth of the Members of the House there will be such charges that this project does not take into account the state of the infrastructure of the tax system in Poland, that the accumulation of several deadlines in one could cause the tax office to be blocked or hinder the work of accountants. Nothing like that. Well, that kind of argument might be valid if we had 1974 or 1984 today, not 2017, when most of the accounts are made in electronic form. Today, few people are going to the tax office with their books filled out by hand, where officials have to check whether they register the data, declarations or documents that affect them. With regard to the services provided to them by accountants, there is nothing to prevent the accountant from establishing, within the framework of established civil law relations with entrepreneurs, that they will account for themselves as they have done so far, i.e. according to this complex system of many deadlines, for many days if this situation is more convenient for those who account for it, and if such a situation is acceptable to entrepreneurs. We do not want to interfere with relations between operators who provide services to entrepreneurs, but we want to give freedom to these entrepreneurs and, above all, to facilitate the system so that it is transparent and citizen-friendly. This is one of the first steps in which we are trying to deal with this difficult matter, as well as with the resistance of state institutions and the resistance of the state apparatus, which has led to a situation where, by complicate the system, for example the tax system, it really makes it a repressive state, hostile to the citizen, unclear to the citizen. It is a country where it is easy to start an economic activity, but it is difficult to do it in practice. So, ladies and gentlemen, I encourage you to support our project, which is an expression, and I stress this once again, economic freedom. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u183	Mrs. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! Tax returns are also to be submitted within the same period. So this is a typical standardization. I would like to say that this harmonisation of deadlines is not contrary to European Union law. The Union does not impose restrictions here, in this respect Member States retain procedural autonomy. As for the evaluation of the project, it seems that it is a simple project, that it is a simple solution. In the initial phase, it will certainly cause some confusion, especially as regards annual accounts, so at the end of December, but ultimately, it seems reasonable and positive to organise the business and reduce its costs. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u185	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Freedom and Solidarnia Circle, I present my position on the proposal for a parliamentary bill amending the Tax Order and amending certain other laws, print no. 1295. The draft envisages amending the Tax Order and other tax and social security laws by harmonising all applicable deadlines for payment of taxes and contributions and time limits for filing tax returns. Applicants propose that for 14 different taxes and danin, among others. PIT, CIT, VAT, ZUS and KRUS contributions, excise duty, introduce one universal deadline for their settlement, which would be the last day of the following month. There are now nine different deadlines for tax reporting obligations, which in the assessment of applicants is detrimental to businesses and authorities, as it unnecessarily complicates the tax system by generating additional costs. Mostly we're talking about the so-called. the costs of compliance, which arise e.g. where an entity submits incomplete tax documentation in order to maintain a short period of time, which calls for additional time to be devoted to the adjustment. The Free and Solidarni Circle is positive, albeit with some significant reservations, referring to the submitted project. It would seem to agree with the position of the National Chamber of Commerce: from the point of view of entrepreneurs, the harmonisation of deadlines for payment of public-law danins should be regarded as favourable, allowing for more flexible planning of work related to the settlement of danin, but provided that the trader does not postpone tax obligations at the last moment, since the accumulation of deadlines on one day may have consequences in the form of late payment of taxes and other danins which have a particularly negative impact on the implementation of the state budget at the end of the year. In addition, the adoption of the last day of the month will have negative consequences for the social security system as it would result in a negative shift in the payment of pension benefits and other benefits granted to citizens from the Social Insurance Fund. It must be objectively recognised that the legal and social interests of these citizens are as important as the interests of entrepreneurs and must be taken into account. At this point, it is necessary to agree with the suggestion made in the opinion to the draft by the National Bank of Poland, that the uniform deadline for the submission of declarations and the settlement of taxes and contributions should be a day earlier than the last day of the following month proposed in the draft. Since the model for applicants is Ireland, where the deadline for settlement is the 23rd day of the following month, it might be worth thinking about introducing a similar one, perhaps even earlier in respect of this month, in the draft law in question. Furthermore, we are considering the possible negative impact of changing the time limits for making declarations and paying excise duties and VAT on the effectiveness of the solutions implemented by the government in order to seal the tax system. For example, according to data submitted a few days ago by the Bankier portal. en adoption of the fuel package introduced in August ub. in the scope of, inter alia, the payment of VAT by the registered consignee within a few days while the obligation to verify and identify the actual customer of the fuel effectively eliminated a significant part of the illicit trade in fuel from the market. According to data from the Ministry of Finance, the increase in legal sales in August 2016 resulted in a reduction in the grey zone, which accounted for around 30% of total consumption in Poland. I ask the applicant's representative to address the question of the relationship between the change in the date of payment of excise duty and VAT and the possible impact of the introduction of this arrangement on the effectiveness of the tax system's sealing. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! Members of the Free and Solidarnia Circle welcome the bill submitted and request that the proposal be submitted for further legislative work. Thank you very much. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u199	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Thank you so much for this bill. Unfortunately, we don't have a government position at this point. I can present the position of the Ministry. The Ministry of Finance clearly recognises the problem of the complexity of tax law. This complexity has many symptoms, manifests itself in many aspects, beginning with the multitude of public danins that citizens and entrepreneurs bear, as well as in the internal complexity of each of these danins, including the fact that tributes are not synchronized with each other. Similar economic events require very different assessments under two taxes, for example in the case of goods and services tax and income tax, the same events require a very large amount of work to be dealt with correctly. Here is the phrase: overregulation or overlaw. Unfortunately, in many laws we have examples of this type of regulation, which are unnecessary, do not perform any important social or public functions, and unfortunately they are raiding, for many years or even many decades they are present in our laws. I would like to say from this point of view that the position of the Ministry is that we are working on the systematic simplification of individual taxes and their internal synchronization, so as to make it easier for entrepreneurs and their financial and accounting services to be better cleared, not to mention the obvious ones. differences between the premium base in the context of the base e.g. personal income tax, which also seems unjustified in many places. Therefore, when you are aware of the obvious complexity and functional deficiencies of individual taxes, it is clear that the time limits for fees and declarations in individual danins paradoxically facilitate the fulfilment of these complex accounts. And paradoxically, the harmonisation of these terms, in such a purely operational, functional, accounting sense, results in a certain accumulation of obligations. I would like to point out that the government is fighting the VAT fraud plague, and there is a new obligation to file a single control file. Entrepreneurs must at this point have sufficient time to prepare these data for submission to the tax authorities so that they can be a high-quality analytical material to eliminate pathology in VAT settlements. Therefore, the introduction of such a direct one-time limit may cause some difficulties, i.e., significant difficulties in the operational tax settlement itself. Thus, the basic benefit of this project - as it was also said here - is the actual deferral of the deadline by several or several days as regards the budgets of individual companies. This is a clear advantage. At the moment, we are analysing the symmetric effects of this because this advantage for businesses to postpone tax payments until the end of the month is associated with a significant budgetary loss, which, if it were to accept this project directly, as it is written, would entail a multi-billion-dollar additional budget cost and the need to find funding to finance additional lending needs of the State. And in that sense, it is difficult to accept in the version in which it is presented. I would like to share this knowledge here that many or most of the time limits for the repayment of bonds are set at the 25th of each month, which in turn is financed by current revenues flowing from CIT and VAT. Therefore, toby needed to find extraordinary resources, to finance the liquidity gap that this project would have created. This would, of course, also lead to technical problems with the settlement of budget units at the turn of the month and to the accumulation of the problem at the end of the year. It would also have to be the answer to the question of who will finance this liquidity gap vis-à-vis local government units, because if we had maintained the current rate of supply of local government units, we again accumulated a negative liquidity effect on the budget by finding additional funds, unless we were also transferring this effect to local government units, which would also have borne part of this cost of the new regulation. In summary, the Ministry of Finance is fully aware of the problem of complex tax law in many of its dimensions. One of the symptoms of this is the multitude of deadlines for declarations and tax payments. It seems to me that a number of these deadlines can be rationalised even in a short period of time, while such a clear and absolute harmonisation of all deadlines at this point will result in significant budgetary perturbations, but we are working on synchronising and simplifying individual taxes so that, perhaps even in the target system, we can talk about one date, as it is in Ireland, as the applicants have put forward here. At this point in time, we believe that, unfortunately, the multitude of these terms paradoxically facilitates the settlement of these main budgetary revenues. Yes, thank you very much. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u203	Dear Marshal, High Izbo! I have the honour to present the draft amendments to the Tax Order Act. Many years of control this is a nightmare of companies, Polish companies. This does not only apply to liabilities to the Social Insurance Institution, but also to tax offices. Recently, a national tax administration has been established - a structure that has absolutely powerful tools to fight dishonest entrepreneurs, dishonest people. We also tried to tighten the penalties for people who participate in VAT carousels. These sanctions will also apply to those who do so unconsciously. We've got a 25-year prison sentence here. It's like a homicide. We also have a tax avoidance clause - a tool that can bring the State's budget due to it in reasonable hands, while in the hands of inexperienced officials or persons acting on someone's order it can pose an absolutely enormous threat to reliable companies that have to deal with unclear, often conflicting rules a day. As if this were not enough, the Polish Sejm is probably one of the most productive sejms in the world. It is a pity - unfortunately - that the number of acts does not go hand in hand with their quality. We've got 31 900 s. of the typewriter for last year. And in this hostile environment, we have a tax limitation institution. The termination of public-law obligations as a result of the expiry of the limitation period means that, despite the failure to meet the needs of the creditor, such as the tax authority, the liability relationship ceases to exist by law. The expiry of the limitation period should take into account ex officio. The purpose of the institutions' limitation to public obligations is to ensure that, after the expiry of the time-limit laid down in the Act, both taxpayers will not be exposed to any action by the authorities aiming at determining and enforcing their tax duties. That's a statement from the justification of this project. The tax limitation institution, on the one hand, disciplines the tax authority for control and, on the other hand, provides a fuse for the taxpayer that it will not endlessly be drawn for the error committed. On the other hand, the current model of tax limitation suspension was challenged by the Constitutional Court in 2015. And this law sort of does the will of the tribunal. The essence of the project is to abolish the possibilities of infinite control, which unfortunately happen. The amendment to this project is intended to increase the motivation of officials to act effectively. For they cannot infinitely lead to an unlimited period of time, if... There's a framework for that. The difference between the project and the current legal situation is the change in the limitation period of tax obligations. Currently we have a 5-year limitation period, without indicating at the same time the time frame within which this time limit should be closed. Proposed amendment, i.e. repeal of § 4, it's like its essence. The current regulation allows the limitation to be interrupted by a preventive enforcement measure. Unfortunately, it happens that procedures are actually initiated just in case. In § 6 In accordance with the proposed amendment, a refund shall be added for a period of not more than 3 years. The revision of this provision will allow for the abolition of unlimited controls which result in increasing tax obligations. In § 7 in point 1 in accordance with the proposed amendment, the applicant shall add a refund no later than 3 years after the date of its initiation. The amendment of this provision will have similar effects to those of the amendment. Article 6 (6) (a) 1. We propose that paragraph 8 be repealed. The repeal of this provision will allow the debtor to dispose freely of the property at the time of the limitation and is in line with the judgment of the Constitutional Court of 8 October 2013. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u211	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the Parliamentary Club of the Polish People's Party, I have the honour to present my position on the parliamentary draft law amending the Tax Order, print no. 1247. The draft law aims to amend the provisions on limitation of tax obligations. Currently, the Tax Order provides for a period of five years of limitation of tax obligations, which may be interrupted whenever enforcement proceedings are taken. The taxable person shall be notified of this proceeding and the tax authority may enforce claims from any new, unknown or previously non-existent assets of the debtor. Enforcement proceedings may therefore be conducted indefinitely and indefinitely. Project applicants propose to extend this 5-year enforcement period for a period of not more than 3 years. As a result of the amendment, a strict period of limitation is laid down and the designers, at the same time repealing § 8 The tax administration makes it possible for the debtor, after that date, to abolish the mortgage collateral and request that the mortgage in question be removed. The projectors, specifying the exact duration of the enforcement procedure, met the ruling of the Constitutional Tribunal. It was the Constitutional Tribunal which stated that the legislator should shape the mechanisms of tax law in such a way that the expiry of the tax obligation would take place within a reasonable period of time and that the enforcement of the tax debt would not last for decades. And it must be said that this initiative by the projectors to try to settle the issue of limitation institutions should be appreciated. On the other hand, taking into account the provisions of the basic law, namely our constitution, it is necessary to approach the issue of limitation of debts much more carefully. They're pointing to it. opinions attached to this project. This is about the opinions of both the Attorney General and the national prosecutor relating to the project solutions. The termination of the tax obligation as a result of the limitation is nevertheless inefficient as it does not satisfy the tax creditor's financial claims. However, with regard to the provisions of the Constitution, it must be pointed out that in Article. 84 of the Constitution is expressed in the principle of tax justice, which consists in the formation of taxes in accordance with the principle of universality and equality. The Constitution obliges everyone to obey and submit to the public burdens imposed on him. The payment of taxes is therefore a constitutional obligation towards the State. Therefore, the introduction of rules promoting taxpayers who fail to comply with tax obligations seems unreasonable, as it leads to a breach of the sense of tax justice from the point of view of the rule of law. The proposal to limit the limitation to 3 years of the limitation period of the tax obligation therefore raises constitutional doubts. The introduction of a pre-defined and automatic limitation period may lead to a weakening of the sense of compliance, even an incentive for unfair debtors to delay and for tax evasion to be calculated. However, while weighing all these arguments, I believe that it is necessary to lean over the institution of limitation, to analyse time, whether the period extended by 3 years is not too short and how to mitigate constitutional rules, and therefore I request on behalf of the Parliamentary Club of the Polish People's Party [[♪ The bell ♪]] to forward the project to the Committee on Public Finance for further work. Thank you. [[Applause]]

The amendments contained in the draft amendment to the Act are intended to cover the period after which the statute of limitations is to take effect. The proposed changes are intended to reduce the time taken by the tax administration from the current 5 to 3 years, while indicating the scope of the closure of the time frame of proceedings also up to 3 years. This will result in the abolition of unlimited controls currently carried out by tax administrations. In addition, the changes will concern, among others. the possibility of removing a mortgage that protects an old-fashioned claim and the time limits for the statute of limitations. The proposed amendment is intended to have tangible positive socio-economic, financial and legal effects, including: is intended to contribute to the stabilisation of the social situation, as well as to preserve the certainty of the legal system in Poland. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u225	Mr. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! I would like to present a draft law which aims to simplify the rules on VAT settlement by Polish companies. For some it's not much, for others it's very much. We propose replacing this declaration with a one-page declaration containing only six fields, which is exactly what EU law requires of us. The proposed amendment is modelled on the solutions adopted in those Member States of the European Union, which have the simplest and most transparent declarations, namely Ireland, where the declaration has only eight fields, and the UK, where the declaration has only nine fields. Where do these problems arise from? A taxable person, especially a VAT taxable person, is required to provide a number of information in the form of reports to the tax authorities. This creates a burden on the taxpayer with disproportionate reporting obligations, which significantly complicates and complicates the rules on the settlement of tax on goods and services in Poland and generates additional costs on the part of Polish entrepreneurs. The Polish taxpayer must provide the tax office with information which is not relevant for the correctness of VAT settlement, information which the tax office has obtained through other declarations, e.g. VAT 27 declarations, information not required in VAT-EU summary information, information not required under Union law, or information already transmitted by certain traders and transmitted by others from 2018 to a single control file. Another simplification that we are proposing in this project is the consolidation of eight different declarations - now seven, because one already does not apply from 1 January - including three different VAT returns, in a single form. The bill repeals the model declarations applicable to special taxes introduced by the governments of the Civic Platform and the Law and Justice, i.e. the tax on the extraction of certain minerals, retail tax and tax on certain financial institutions. According to this proposal, the amounts of tax for the above-mentioned new danins will be reported by companies in the VAT return. In other words, our bill introduces one uncomplicated form in place of the seven existing VAT return forms: VAT-7, VAT-7K, VAT-7D - withdrawn - VAT-26, P-KOP/MS, P-KOP/RG, FIN-1 and PSD-1. Moreover, this draft amendment to the Act on VAT on goods and services proposes one more simplification of VAT deduction on expenditure relating to cars. Of course, we realize... The draft law does not establish or provide a form for the final VAT return. It shall be included in the section on justification. We know that the preparation of such a new declaration will be the responsibility of the Ministry of Finance, but for our part we declare full cooperation in preparing, refining, consulting the new declaration with the Ministry of Finance so that Poles can live better so that their business is conducted simpler and cheaper. The amendment proposed by us will have a positive social, economic and financial impact, in particular among VAT taxpayers, who will be relieved of their time-consuming and costly obligations regarding the fulfilment of long and complex declarations forms. The time and resources saved in this way will be available to entrepreneurs to develop their business. Who's gonna be helped by this project? Everything. Of course, current entrepreneurs are a little accustomed to these complex regulations, but it will ease them a little bit, maybe they can pay their accountants a little less, they will bear a little less cost. On the other hand, as I said at the outset, this is a huge opportunity for all young people - young and not only - who want to start an economic activity in Poland, but complicated rules represent a huge barrier to entry. We want to eliminate this barrier. I would like to conclude by referring to some of the arguments against which may fall in this Chamber. I think that if I refer to them now, I will make it a little easier for you: there will be less entry, descent and perhaps some arguments, things that you can raise, I will explain at the outset. One such argument is that the project could jeopardise the sealing of the VAT system. I disagree with that argument. The more simplified the declaration procedure is, the easier it is to find a mistake or a case of deliberate misrepresentation of officials. Moreover, the Ministry of Finance is carrying out activities aimed at sealing the VAT system much more advanced than the issue of the VAT return. We have, as I said before, a uniform control file through which all information can be transmitted to the tax office. There's a lot of talk about different methods of fighting VAT carousels, split payment. The simplification of the VAT return certainly does not affect VAT carousels, it will not make it difficult for the Ministry of Labour to fight frauds, but will make life easier for Polish entrepreneurs. Another argument is that the project does not provide effective control. Well, the simpler the declaration, the more effective the control. Officials who have any doubts will always be able to call on the taxpayer to provide explanations to the tax authority. Another argument is that the Ministry of Finance plans to review the declaration in the future. That's why we're coming up with this proposal. Why wait? I propose that on the basis of our project, the Kukiz15 project, we begin this extensive work to simplify the other declarations as well. I propose not to reject the project at first reading, to refer it to the committee, to work with the Ministry on it and that it be such a first step in simplifying the tax system in Poland. One, a little absurd, argument that appears is that other countries have even more fields to fill, e.g. Italy has 586 fields and Hungary has 99 fields. Ladies and gentlemen, let's go after the best, not the downside with the other weaklings. Ireland also has only 8 fields, and the European Union has no doubt about the method of accounting for this country. Let us strive for the best solutions. Moreover, I would like Poland to be a pioneer in terms of simple law, even ahead of such countries as Ireland and the United Kingdom. There was also doubt about adding other taxes to the VAT return. In our view, this is easier: fewer papers, fewer trees, the settlement of several taxes on a single declaration is simpler. But of course, if, as part of the consultation with companies, the aim is to separate it, then okay - the most important thing is to simplify the VAT declaration. Last argument: costs of changes in information systems. These costs will still occur if these announcements of the review of the declaration are fulfilled. IT costs are always borne anyway, this should never be an argument in the case of changes to the law for the better. I would like to add that this project has also been evaluated by many institutions. Institutions such as the National Bank of Poland and the National Bank of Poland and KRS have not made any comments about this project, and the Business Centre Club has given us a very positive opinion on this project, which we are so pleased about - we are so pleased that we can meet the expectations of the environment associated with Polish entrepreneurs. So, High Izbo, Minister, we are here declaring cooperation with the Ministry of Finance to simplify taxes, and I call on the High House to support this project to give it some oxygen and enable it to work in the finance committee. Let's make life easier for entrepreneurs. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

Proposals also propose simplifying the rules for taxpayers who want to deduct 100% VAT on cars used exclusively for business purposes, eliminating the current obligation to notify this fact within 7 days of the first expenditure related to the vehicle and introducing only an obligation to inform the use of a 100% deduction of VAT in a monthly or quarterly declaration. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u239	Mr. Marshal! Minister! High Izbo! The main objective of the bill is to simplify VAT accounting rules, VAT by Polish taxpayers. One of the proposals is to change the way the tax authority communicates information about motor vehicles used exclusively for business activities for which taxpayers are required to keep records of the course of the vehicle. At present, taxpayers shall provide information about vehicles within 7 days of the date on which they incurred the first expenditure related to these vehicles. The draft law proposes that this information be provided in the tax return for the period during which taxpayers will bear the first expenditure related to these vehicles. At the same time, the project repeals the obligation to update information on these vehicles in case of changes e.g. the use of the car by the taxpayer. However, the most important change introduced by this proposal is the simplification of the current model VAT return. The current declaration form - this has already been highlighted by the applicants - has two pages and contains 60 boxes. Here it is proposed to replace this declaration with a 1-page declaration containing only six fields. The designers justify that these provisions result from EU VAT Directive 2006/112/EC, which only requires six fields in force in the declaration. The next amendment proposed in this project is the consolidation of eight different declarations, including four different VAT only declarations, in a single form. According to the draft law, all VAT payers will submit information on a single model declaration, which will be introduced by the Finance Minister's regulation and is an integral part of the draft law. The draft law also repeals model tax returns other than VAT, including excavation tax, retail tax or tax on certain financial institutions. Therefore, in summary, it must be said that the proposal in question contains indeed very broad simplifications, which, as part of the reduction of bureaucracy, deserve an in-depth analysis by the High House. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u251	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! The Commission for the Protection of the Environment, Natural Resources and Forestry at its meeting on 23 February 2017 examined the draft law amending the Environmental Law in print No 1127. The project was submitted to the marshal's staff on December 5th. It was sent to first reading on 12 December. During that first reading, which took place on 26 January, a request was made for rejection at first reading. However, this proposal has not been accepted by the High House, and the Committee on the Environment, Natural Resources and Forestry is presenting a report in print No 1318 after the work has been carried out. High Izbo! The proposal under discussion today amends the article. 400d, art. 400f and Article. 400j Act Environmental Law, which governs the establishment of selected bodies of the National Fund for Environmental Protection and Water Management and of the Regional Environmental Protection and Water Management Funds. The amendment aims to reduce the costs of their operation and to simplify and speed up the procedures for the appointment of their members and thus to improve the work of these bodies. According to the proposed regulations, there will be a reduction in the number: firstly, the supervisory board of the national environmental fund from 12 to 11 members, while the local government side of the Commission of the Joint Government and the Territorial Self-Government will have one representative; secondly, the supervisory boards of the regional environmental and water funds from seven to five members; and thirdly, the management board of the regional environmental and water funds - instead of three to five, it is planned to reduce to a maximum of two people. This change leads to the rationalisation of the costs of functioning of voivodship environmental and water funds. I would like to remind you that currently the management board of 16 regional environmental funds consists of 54 members, including 16 presidents and 38 vice presidents. On average, the fund's management consists of three or four persons, with funds, e.g. in Katowice, where the number of board members is up to five. In comparison, the national management of the fund has four members who have many times more money to distribute and re-order. Currently, the President's monthly salary is on average over 20,000. gross zloty. The highest stakes are about 25,000. zł. For this reason, the total cost of the remuneration of the regional management boards of environmental and water funds is about PLN 11 million per year. To this they are mandatory, ladies and gentlemen, annual prizes, which are difficult to estimate, but four times a month's salary, monthly salary is standard. Thus, the planned change in environmental legislation, consisting of a reduction to two management posts, and of course the planned change in remuneration, about 17 000. PLN for the president and less than 13 thousand. for the vice president, it will reduce management costs by about 5700 thousand. PLN per year. In addition, there is a reduction in the number of members of the supervisory boards from seven to five, resulting in additional savings of around 1200 000. PLN per year. Let me remind you that the average salary of a member of the board is about PLN 3100 gross. So together, ladies and gentlemen, savings will amount to about PLN 7 million per year. Ladies and gentlemen, for this amount, one treatment plant, medium size, for about 15,000. People can stand up peacefully, and I think about 1,000. fifth-generation furnaces, those that will improve air protection. I think therefore that the game is really worth taking. High Izbo! The measure of effectiveness of the voivodship funds is, among others. such parameter: the cost of payment of 1 PLN of co-financing. For the national fund, this indicator amounts to 2.5 gr per 1 PLN of co-financing, while the average value of this indicator for voivodship funds reaches 5.5 gr, i.e. more than twice as much. However, there are voivodship funds for record-breakers, e.g. Western Pomerania, for which the cost of payment of 1 PLN of co-financing reaches almost 12 gr, or the Podlaskie Fund, where it exceeds 13 gr. It's time to stop, ladies and gentlemen. Voivodship funds should be used to support measures to improve the environment rather than to maintain the son. High Izbo! Amendments to Article 400f of the Act aims to simplify and speed up procedures for the appointment of members of the supervisory boards of voivodship funds for environmental protection and water management. Currently, the procedure for their appointment is inconsistent, complex and long-term, and in practice leads to situations which may raise very serious doubts. Act in Art. 400f paragraph. 2 provides that the supervisory boards of the voivodship funds shall consist of persons with specific functions, persons selected by the sejmik from among certain categories of candidates and persons notified or appointed by certain entities. Moreover, the legislator has granted powers to appoint and dismiss members of the supervisory boards of the funds to voivodships and, at the same time, the right to dismiss the minister responsible for environmental matters who dismiss the members of the supervisory boards if they have ceased to fulfil the functions set out in the Act. The process of reporting and appointment of members of supervisory boards is complicated, and since it is complemented by the formation of a voivodship by a seymik, i.e. by a body whose sessions are usually convened once a month, there is a very serious time gap between these activities. The shortcomings of the current rules are particularly evident in the case of withdrawal of the notification and withdrawal of the designation of the person in the composition of the supervisory board and the simultaneous notification and appointment of a new person. In such a situation, the process of changing the composition of the supervisory board may take up to 2 and 3 months, with obvious doubts as to the legitimacy of the supervisory board member's activities, whose notification or appointment has been withdrawn. The solution proposed in this project today, i.e. the granting of the Minister responsible for Human Rights. the environment of competence to appoint and remove the members of the supervisory boards of the Funds, enables a smooth, without delay and doubt, change the composition of the supervisory boards of the funds. The purpose of the solution contained in paragraph 1. Article 8 400f, i.e. to grant the Minister responsible for the environment of competence to appoint its representatives in place of members of councils not designated by the voivode, the national management board of the fund or the sejmik, is the discipline of the said entities to effectively appoint members of the councils and enable the supervisory boards of the funds to operate smoothly. A similar nature with regard to the appointment of the management boards of the Funds shall be the solution contained in Article. 400 lips. 2a, according to which, if the management board of the voivodship does not appoint members of the management board within 7 days in accordance with the proposal of the supervisory board of the fund, the appointment of members of the management board shall be carried out by the supervisory board of the fund. High Izbo! Art. 2 of the project provides that the mandate of the former members of the supervisory boards of the voivodship fund and the national fund shall expire on the date of the entry into force of this law. In turn, the article. 3 requires the appointment of members and vice-chairmen of the regional supervisory boards of the funds by the voivodships, the management board of the national fund and the seymika of voivodships within 30 days of the entry into force of the amendment of the legislation, and after 30 days the minister will appoint members of the supervisory boards of the voivodship environmental funds. Further transitional provisions lay down the deadlines for the request by the Provincial Supervisory Board of Funds to the Management Board of the Voivodship to appoint members of the Management Boards of Regional Environmental and Water Management Funds, the deadline for the Management Boards of the Voivodship to appoint these members of the Boards of Boards of the WFOŚ indicated in the proposal and the deadline for the expiry of the mandates of the existing members of the Boards of the Voivodship Boards of Environmental and Water Management Funds. Art. The 4th project establishes a 14-day vacathio legis. High Izbo! The Committee on the Environment, Natural Resources and Forestry shall also submit, at the request of the applicants, six minority proposals. High Izbo! On behalf of the Committee on the Environment, Natural Resources and Forestry, I request the adoption of a draft amendment to the Environmental Law in print No 1318. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]

A similar distinction exists among environmental authorities, which are alongside the Minister responsible for Environment. The environment is also a mound, mayor or president of the city, starosta, sejmik voivodship, marshal of voivodship or voivodship. However, the fact that voivodship funds are local legal entities does not make them local organisational units. This fact has specific consequences. We still believe that it is wrong to organise the composition of supervisory boards from the same persons who perform environmental functions in different institutions. There is no doubt, of course, that the supervisory boards must be composed of competent persons. However, it should not necessarily be the directors of other environmental institutions or possibly their alternates. The supervisory boards as a fund control body must be extremely transparent, and there must not even be a shadow of suspicion of bias or any, even unpersonal, interest. Now on the issue of changes in the composition of the management of the funds, I will repeat that there are no obstacles for the management of even a large company or a public company to be carried out on a one-man basis, although in practice, for example. in various types of companies is that it is carried out on a double basis. It is from practice that this is an optimal number. It should also be borne in mind that an increase in the number of members of supervisory boards must have a particular reason, e.g. the protection of the rights of minority shareholders. In the case of voivodship environmental funds, it is difficult to see why the composition of supervisory boards would consist of more than five people. It should therefore be believed that the reduction in the number of environmental fund managers and their supervisors is not only intended to save money from unnecessary duplication of positions in these bodies, but that it is primarily about improving their performance. Ladies and gentlemen of the clubs and opposition circles, with a little bit of goodwill, you will understand that the proposed amendment to the Environmental Law Act both simplifies and rationalises the procedures for the appointment of the management board and members of the supervisory boards of the regional environmental funds, as well as aims to improve the functioning of these bodies. This is the thought that accompanies us all the time working on this project, and therefore, noticing a few minor inconsistencies, without changing the main assumptions of the project, I am authorized to make five amendments that clarify the draft law. Once again, I stress that we are certainly not dealing with an attempt on democracy in the exercise of the Law and Justice, but with common sense, and that is why, on behalf of the Law and Justice Club, I ask once again, ladies and gentlemen, to accept the solutions recommended to them here, along with the amendments. Environmental law Voivodship environmental and water protection funds are local legal persons within the meaning of Article. 9 point 14 of the Public Finance Act of 27 August 2009. Reference to the Article. 9 point 14 of the Public Finance Act means that the voivodship funds are local legal entities set up on the basis of a separate law to perform public tasks belonging to the public finance sector. At the same time, Art. 400. 3 Environmental protection laws provide that voivodship environmental funds are not voivodship local organizational units referred to in Article. Article 8 (1) (b) and (c) of Regulation (EU) No 1303/2013 1 Act of 5 June 1998 on the local government of the voivodship, i.e. are not voivodship organizational units created by the local government in order to perform the tasks of the voivodship. This is a very, very important thing. Such a solution is appropriate, among others. the position of administrative courts. In its judgment of 13 January 2005, the Court of Justice held that: NSA... I'm not gonna read it here because it's a pretty long sentence, but if I have to, I'll give it to you in writing. It is also important to analyse the catalogue of its tasks to determine the status of the voivodship environmental fund. And so according to the article. 400b. 2 Environmental protection laws The objective of the voivodship environmental fund is to finance environmental protection and water management within the scope set out in Article. 400a paragraph. Paragraph 1 of that Law and paragraphs 1 to 11 to 42 of that Law. For the purpose of the operation of the voivodship fund, it is also necessary to create the conditions for the implementation of environmental financing and water management, in particular by providing support for actions for this implementation and its promotion, as well as through cooperation with other entities, including local government units, entrepreneurs and entities established outside the Republic of Poland. In turn, the analysis of the article. 400. 1 Environmental law leads to the conclusion that projects or tasks in the field of environmental protection and water management, to which funding is called for by voivodship funds, are largely implemented by government administration - e.g. developing water management plans, carrying out research and measurements by the Environmental Protection Inspection or Trade Inspection - support the development of technologies for environmental protection and water management or education or relate to activities of a particular type, e.g. the restoration of protected plant and animal species. It can therefore be concluded that the essence of the activities of the voivodship funds is not to finance the tasks of the local government, but to provide financial support for the implementation of certain categories of projects aimed at protecting the environment and water management... [[Exactly.]] ...executed by different actors. This is justified by the legislator's acceptance in Article. 400. 3 B.C. solution according to which voivodship environmental funds are not voivodship organizational units created by the local government. Nor are they the authorities of the local government. They are created on the basis of only environmental law. Therefore, it follows from the provisions cited above that although the voivodship funds perform public tasks, they are not public authorities - I also wanted to emphasize very strongly. So it's not an attack on the local government. I still wanted to say, because it was a reference to the article. 15 and Article 15 of Regulation (EU) No 182/2011 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 16 February 2011 laying down the rules and general principles concerning mechanisms for control by Member States of the Commission's exercise Referring to the possibility of infringement by the proposed law laid down in Article. 165 The Constitution of the Republic of Poland's principle of legal personality of local government should, in principle, be raised by the same arguments, for example, because this principle is closely linked to the principle of decentralisation of public authority and the principle of autonomy of local self-government. If the legal personality of local government units in the field of public law is related to the public mission of local government to participate in the exercise of public authority, it is difficult to recognise that the proposed act violates this personality, since it does not change the scope of the tasks of the local government in the field of environmental protection and water management. We do not change the tasks of the local government. Nor does any other environmental and water management tasks change at the level of district government or local government. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u329	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! I have the honour to present a report by the Committee on Economic Affairs and Development and the Committee on Public Finance on the Government's draft law amending certain laws in order to facilitate the recovery of claims, print No 1185. The joint committees have chosen from their composition Members who have sat in the subcommittee to draw up the project. The subcommittee held its meetings on 9 and 21 and 23 February 2017 and requests its adoption. The joint committees that the subcommittee's report adopted yesterday also request adoption. It is included in print No 1339. The project itself is contained in package plans, a plan for responsible development, 100 changes for companies - a package of facilitations for entrepreneurs and aims to facilitate the recovery of claims. The proposed solutions are intended to: improve the operation of business information offices, create a register of public claims, amend the investor's liability for liabilities to the subcontractor in the construction process by modifying the content of the article. 647 Civil Code, specify the grounds justifying the conclusion of a settlement by the operator of public funds in disputes concerning civil claims, raise the upper limit of the value of the subject-matter of the dispute for matters recognised in simplified proceedings, introduce legislative changes in group proceedings, increase the effectiveness of safeguards and enforcement proceedings. During the work of the committee, the subcommittee decided to adopt part of the editorial and legislative amendments and substantive amendments, such as a change whereby the creditor would not be obliged to send a notification of the risk of disclosure in the register to the obliged party if the amount of the commitment was very low, i.e. less than four times the cost of warning. The current cost of notice is PLN 11.60. This solution prevents irrational expenditure of tax offices and local government units. As regards the amendment to waive the method of making available to the parties the amount of debt in a certain quota range, the quota range provides only an approximate knowledge of the size of public debt. The principle has been introduced that the person concerned is given information on the actual amount of the debt owed at the date of verification in the register. The next amendment refers to the conditions for obtaining consumer liability data from the Register of Public Claims. According to the project, obtaining such a debtor's data from the register requires his written authorisation. However, the draft does not lay down rules for obtaining such data by economic information offices and institutions set up pursuant to Article. 105 Bank law. In view of the fact that these entities act on behalf of and for the benefit of other persons, their clients interested in obtaining debtor data, the requirement to obtain authorisation to check the consumer in the register should therefore apply to the entity at which the abovementioned order was made. offices and institutions will operate. On the other hand, the office itself should be required to take a statement from the payer that it has a valid written authorisation from the consumer. The proposed solution takes into account the aspect of the technical solutions used in the activities of the above mentioned. offices and institutions, i.e. the widespread use of electronic communications. Amendments to Article 3 and Article 4 of Regulation (EU) No 182/2011 of the European Parliament and of the Council [3] shall apply mutatis mutandis. 5a are linked to an amendment to Article. 18o, which concerns the imposition on economic information offices and institutions established pursuant to Article. 105 Bank law requires the customer to receive a statement on the consumer's authorisation to obtain data from the register. The proposal provides for a sanction for requests to make the data available to the obliged consumer without its authorisation. In order to ensure consistency of system liability, an action should also be subject to the obligation to obtain a statement from the entity for which the data are sought in the register of possession of such an authorisation. Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! On behalf of the joint committees, I request that the bill be adopted together with the amendments proposed and accepted by the committees. Thank you. [[Applause]]

ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u331	Mrs. Marshal! High Seym! I would like to present the opinion of the Parliamentary Club Law and Justice on the case submitted by the Joint Parliamentary Committees to print a draft law, in short, on the recovery of claims. This project is, of course, part of a broader government plan to facilitate business activity and may not be as spectacular as the others, which will soon be in the Sejm, but it is a non-controversial law. The regulation of this draft law significantly eliminates the nuisance of the examination of confidence in market participants and the amendments made during the committee's work have further improved the proposed changes to the rules. We have also made the necessary editorial and legislative adjustments. As you know, trust in other economic players is a key factor in the functioning of companies, the level of solvency of the entrepreneur and its liquidity depend on the timely regulation of liabilities by counterparties. An important new element of this law is the creation of a register of public-law commitments, thus enabling the Offices to verify economic information whether the entity concerned, for example. he doesn't owe taxes. This will allow a more thorough examination of the counterparties and thus protect honest entrepreneurs from falling into debt spirals. This problem is particularly burdensome for hundreds of thousands of micro-entrepreneurs who cannot afford to rent expensive law firms and long-term legal proceedings. As a result, the law we adopt will also reduce the need to maintain a high level of reserve in case of emergency, i.e. to improve liquidity and the speed of money circulation in the economy in general. Companies will have more money for development, for investment, for employment of new workers, and finally for innovative activities. The project also aims to improve legal proceedings in economic matters, thus avoiding the long-term recovery situation in the long term. Creditors will be better informed about the solvency of the debtor through this Act. This will in particular improve the activity in sectors particularly affected by the problem of non-regulation of invoices, especially in the construction sector, where the level of debt in listed companies alone exceeds PLN 4 billion. The Act will help to reduce the time taken by courts to deal with economic matters by reducing the need for judicial redress. At present, the data on time limits for court settlement is alarming. In 2015, the average time taken by courts to deal with economic matters was 14 months, in district courts more than 40% of business cases were dealt with for more than one year, in turn more than 25% - for more than 2 years, 10% cases - over 3 years. It doesn't take much imagination to realize what it means not to recover due payment for a sold item or service of considerable value for a small company that doesn't have enough money to survive a difficult time when money is being recovered. I worked for free and I have to go bankrupt - it is a sad reality of many thousands of Polish entrepreneurs. Of course, the bill in question makes sense when it is accompanied by the government or by other solutions announced by Prime Minister Morawiecki: facilitating the reduction of burdens on the Social Security Office for micro-enterprises, exemption from bureaucratic obligations for the smallest companies, tax exemptions for innovation or accelerated depreciation at a higher level than at present. We hope, in fact, that the government bill worked so thoroughly by the Sejm committees will contribute to a significant improvement in economic life in our country. We will, of course, vote in favour of the proposed project as a club of Law and Justice. Thank you for your attention. [[Applause]]

The actions of a similar nature were many, until finally came the time when the land of Łódź, Piotrkowska and Częstochowa were liberated from German occupation as a result of the winter offensive of the Red Army. This action, as we know, did not bring the expected freedom, but another slavery - the Soviet slavery. This was a difficult time for Polish soldiers. As a result of the analysis of the situation on 19 January 1945 in Częstochowa, which since October 1944 was the seat of the Home Army, General Leopold Okulicki pseudoud. The bear gave the order to disband the Home Army. Lieutenant Wiesław Janusiak as the commanding officer of Warsaw remembered his credo well: As long as there is even the slightest hope and the ability to demonstrate to Russia that its politics is vile, swine and possessive, and the fight against its imperialism and the taking away of the eastern territories of Poland, it is our duty, even at the cost of great casualties, to continue in this fight. The National Army and its supporters are about 60% of Polish society: who is our enemy - is the enemy of Poland. Lieutenant Wiesław Janusiak's thoughts in the above credo have always accompanied decision making, so despite the propaganda assurances of the Polish security forces about the possibility of returning to normal life he did not even try to come out of hiding because he knew what was waiting for such as him. So he didn't put up a gun in 1945 after the German occupation ended. He stayed hidden. At the turn of May and June 1945, as one of the first field commanders in the presence of Warsaw, he was sworn in by the pseudonym Prawdzic. From now on, when he has been entrusted with the position of commander of the Self-Defense Service and Protection of the Society of the battalion code name of the Reaper, he operates in the district of Piotrków Trybunalski. As of April 1946, he has been appointed commander of the above-mentioned Brony Company battalion and is subsequently entrusted with the post of Deputy Commander of the Lt. Jerzy Jasinski's pseudo. Janusz in the Second Conspiracy Command of the Polish Army and the head of SOS. It is difficult to replace all functions in a short time [[♪ The bell ♪]] he was a member of the CWP. Wiesław Janusiak. ParlaMint-PL_2017-03-08-sejm-37-1.u355	Mrs. Marshal! High Izbo! Thank you so much for agreeing to speak. Ladies and gentlemen! I would like to use this time to draw attention to the role of cultural creators in the functioning of modern societies. Unfortunately, culture is treated as a cost all the time, not as an investment, which is in fact. The subject of culture as an element of social life penetrates the media almost exclusively in crisis situations, that is, in connection with some scandal, or in connection with the protests of the creators facing cuts in funds. Culture is a value, a measurable value and a percentage of a particular profit. In the global market, we are distinguished by our unique culture. Either we take care of it, cherish it and profit from it, or lose a valuable competitive advantage. We often talk about the knowledge economy, sustainable development. Without the support and promotion of culture, we can forget about it. If we don't believe in it, we'll never break the glass ceiling. Therefore, I would like to mention the Regional Association of Cultural Developers in my family beautiful Rzeszow. The association, which was founded in 1980 on the wave of solidarity movements, set itself the goal of promoting a higher culture in the working groups. It was found in a new reality, attracting people of culture who wanted to preserve their folk and national traditions. In 2011, the association established cooperation with the City Centre for Culture in Lviv. It operates in almost all cultural institutions: choirs, creative unions, communal cultural centres, clubs, cemeteries, libraries, schools and kindergartens. We have to take care of people like that. We need to make sure that the passionate of history, the amateurs of ethnography, local artists feel that they have the support of us. The work they do is treated as a free mission. If, as a country, we do not remove the barriers facing grassroots movements that promote culture, we will lose a great deal. Culture can be revived by grants and subsidies, but a real living culture requires freedom and independence. This is our treasure. Let's not sell it for nothing. The association was founded in 1980 on a wave of solidarity movements and was called the Workers' Association of Cultural Developers. It had workers in its ranks and set itself the goal of promoting a working culture. Like every organization, it went through its ups and downs. Following the crisis in 2007, the association was reorganized. In 2009, when it turned out that there were no workers in the RSTK ranks, the name was changed to the Regional Association of Cultural Developers, and the aim was not only to promote working culture, but to promote culture in general. The following sections were created: literary, whose director was Jerzy Nawrocki, musical, directed by Zygmunt Kiełbowicz, plastic with Emilia Wołoszyn, pedagogical and teaching with Zofia Krawiec and Katarzyna Błaszczuk, photo and film with Alicja Borowiec, Władysław Wiąck and Józef Homa, live words with Dorota Kwoka and Stachem Ożog and folklore led by Stefania Buda and Kazimierz Wiśniowski. The Association has its Information Flow Centre, headed by Katarzyna Pajdak, and cooperates with the media and abroad through Regina Nachacz. The association's achievements also consist of publishing activities: two editions of the international competition In 2011 it was created by the young RSTKs. The Association publishes a newsletter entitled "Culture and Song," in which the works of members and sympathizers of the RSTK are published. Since the 4th issue of 2011, the newsletters have been extended to include the Young People's Contribution. This contribution is edited by a student at the Jagiellonian University and the State Higher Theatrical School of Benjamin Bukowski, and co-workers are young people from Podkarpacie studying at the universities known in the country: Jagiellonian University, Górniczo-Hutnicza Academy, Wrocław University, Warsaw University and Poznań University. In 2011 cooperation with the urban cultural centre from Lviv was established. The activities of the members of our association are visible in almost all cultural institutions: choirs, creative unions, communal cultural centres, clubs, cemeteries, libraries, schools, kindergartens, etc. In 2013, the association received the award of the Management Board of Podkarpackie Voivodship for its entire activities. From this point of view, I would like to thank you very much for the actions taken by the Regional Association of Cultural Developers from Rzeszów. I wish you more success. See you next time. Thank you very much. [[Applause]]
